Statistics that the column type does not carry are\n// left blank.\nfunc profileRow(name string, values []string) []string {\n\ttyp := profile.InferType(values)\n\tcounts := profile.Counts(values)\n\textremes := profile.MinMax(typ, values)\n\trow := []string{\n\t\tname,\n\t\ttyp,\n\t\tstrconv.Itoa(counts.Rows),\n\t\tstrconv.Itoa(counts.Nulls),\n\t\tstrconv.Itoa(counts.Distinct),\n\t\textremeText(typ, extremes.Min, extremes.MinRat),\n\t\textremeText(typ, extremes.Max, extremes.MaxRat),\n\t\t\"\", \"\", \"\",\n\t}\n\tif sum, mean, ok := profile.SumMean(typ, values); ok {\n\t\trow[7] = sumText(typ, sum)\n\t\trow[8] = profile.FormatDecimal(mean)\n\t}\n\tif median, ok := profile.Median(typ, values); ok {\n\t\trow[9] = profile.FormatDecimal(median)\n\t}\n\treturn row\n}\n// extremeText renders a minimum or maximum. A header-only table is valid.\nfunc Validate(t *Table) error {\n\tif t.Header == nil {\n\t\treturn &RejectedError{Reason: \"no header record: input is empty or blank\"}\n\t}\n\n\tseen := make(map[string]bool, len(t.Header))\n\tfor i, name := range t.Header {\n\t\tif name == \"\" {\n\t\t\treturn &RejectedError{Reason: fmt.Sprintf(\"header column %d is empty\", i+1)}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif seen[name] {\n\t\t\treturn &RejectedError{Reason: fmt.Sprintf(\"header name %q is repeated\", name)}\n\t\t}\n\t\tseen[name] = true\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, rec := range t.Records {\n\t\tif len(rec.Fields) != len(t.Header) {\n\t\t\treturn &RejectedError{\n\t\t\t\tLine:   rec.Line,\n\t\t\t\tReason: fmt.Sprintf(\"record has %d fields, header has %d\", len(rec.Fields), len(t.Header)),\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\npackage csvdata\n\nimport \"strings\"\n\n// Trim removes leading and trailing spaces (U+0020) from every header name and\n// every record field, in place.
